I don't think I do.\n\nDoctor: Do you have trouble focusing or getting distracted in different situations, like at work, at home, or in social settings?\n\nPatient: Um... I guess so. Yeah, it happens at home a lot. And... when I'm out with friends sometimes, I guess. I don't really know about work right now, since I don't have a job. But yeah, it's just... around.\n\nDoctor: Do you think your trouble with focusing and getting distracted is causing problems for you in your daily life?\n\nPatient: Um... yeah, I guess so. Like... I've been trying to get into this automotive technician thing. But I just... I don't know, I keep missing the training deadlines. And I've had to redo my applications a bunch of times because of it. So I'm just... not really moving forward with it at all right now. Even though I know I can handle the hands-on work fine. It's just... yeah.\n\nDoctor: Did you have trouble sitting still or fidgeting when you were a kid?\n\nPatient: Um... yeah, I guess. I mean, even back in elementary school, my teachers were always... moving my desk around. Because I'd talk out of turn, or I'd forget my homework. And... I guess I'd just rush through the worksheets and make a bunch of careless mistakes. So... yeah, it was kind of like that back then, too.\n\nDoctor: Were you often described as 'on the go' or having an abundance of energy when you were a child?\n\nPatient: Um... I don't really know. Just... couldn't sit still, I guess.\n\nDoctor: Did you have trouble paying attention or following instructions when you were a kid?\n\nPatient: Um... yeah. Kind of like I was saying a minute ago... about them always moving my seat. I just... I guess I didn't really listen when they gave out the homework, so I'd forget it. And... I didn't really follow the instructions on those worksheets either. I'd just rush through them and make mistakes. So... yeah.\n\nDoctor: Did you have any of these problems with focusing, fidgeting, or getting distracted when you were a teenager?\n\nPatient: Um... I don't really remember high school that well. I guess it was probably just... more of the same. I don't think I do.\n\nDoctor: Do you have trouble focusing or getting distracted in different situations, like at work, at home, or in social settings?\n\nPatient: Um... I guess so. Yeah, it happens at home a lot. And... when I'm out with friends sometimes, I guess. I don't really know about work right now, since I don't have a job. But yeah, it's just... around.\n\nDoctor: Do you think your trouble with focusing and getting distracted is causing problems for you in your daily life?\n\nPatient: Um... yeah, I guess so. Like... I've been trying to get into this automotive technician thing. But I just... I don't know, I keep missing the training deadlines. And I've had to redo my applications a bunch of times because of it. So I'm just... not really moving forward with it at all right now. Even though I know I can handle the hands-on work fine. It's just... yeah.\n\nDoctor: Did you have trouble sitting still or fidgeting when you were a kid?\n\nPatient: Um... yeah, I guess. I mean, even back in elementary school, my teachers were always... moving my desk around. Because I'd talk out of turn, or I'd forget my homework. And... I guess I'd just rush through the worksheets and make a bunch of careless mistakes. So... yeah, it was kind of like that back then, too.\n\nDoctor: Were you often described as 'on the go' or having an abundance of energy when you were a child?\n\nPatient: Um... I don't really know. I don't remember people saying that exactly. I mean, I moved around a lot, like I said, but... I don't know if they called it having a lot of energy. Just... couldn't sit still, I guess.\n\nDoctor: Did you have trouble paying attention or following instructions when you were a kid?\n\nPatient: Um... yeah. Kind of like I was saying a minute ago... about them always moving my seat. I just... I guess I didn't really listen when they gave out the homework, so I'd forget it. And... I didn't really follow the instructions on those worksheets either. I'd just rush through them and make mistakes. So... yeah.\n\nDoctor: Did you have any of these problems with focusing, fidgeting, or getting distracted when you were a teenager?\n\nPatient: Um... I don't really remember high school that well. I guess it was probably just... more of the same. I didn't really pay attention to it, to be honest.\n\nDoctor: Have you experienced these problems with focusing and getting distracted at school, at work, and at home?\n\nPatient: Um... I guess so. Yeah. It's just kind of... everywhere, I suppose. I don't really keep track of where it happens most. It's just... how it is.\n\nDoctor: Did you have any of these problems with focusing, fidgeting, or getting distracted before you started elementary school?\n\nPatient: Um... I really don't know.
